Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a geometric algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 340. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 252/4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to geometric structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 340/252 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 219.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let the geometric structure S satisfy the conditions of optimality condition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 443. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 259 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).
